#revolt chance in the specified region if it is fully controlled:
#Yugoslavia		#40% per 10 days or 1.2 times per month
#Russia			#133% per 10 days or 4.0 times per month (divided into two events, 66% each). Russia is a VERY large region, and even in bitter peace, Germany does not control all it
#Belarus		#40% per 10 days or 1.2 times per month
#Ukraine		#18% per 10 days or 0.5 times per month
#Greece/Albania	#18% per 10 days or 0.5 times per month
#Poland			#18% per 10 days or 0.5 times per month
#Caucasus		#18% per 10 days or 0.5 times per month (very favorable terrain, but some population groups may be sympathetic
#France			#13% per 10 days or 0.4 times per month (Germany only controls all of France after Vichy is gone)
#Spain			#40% per 10 days or 1.2 times per month

#northern russia? - has good terrain but population is too sparse
#Turkey?
#UK?

#Since random is not actually random, options are limited. 
#Random is only calculated once per event/gametime, so probability within the same event is NOT multiplicative

#Yugoslavia
event = {
    id = 4000
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = YUG } }
			ispuppet = YUG
			random = 25 #limit to 25
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
			area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
			area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
			area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
		}
		OR = {
			random = 10 
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 34
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 37
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 40
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 27
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "Partisans in Yugoslavia!"
	desc = "Partisans in Yugoslavia!"
	
	action = {
		name = "Revolt in Banja Luka!"
		trigger = { OR = { control = { province = 383 data = -3 } control = { province = 383 data = REB } } }
		ai_chance = 3
		command = { type = revolt which = 383 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 383 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 383 when = 1 }
		command = { type = setflag which = revolt_count_YUG when = 1 value = 1 }
	}

event = {
    id = 4001
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = RUS } }
			ispuppet = RUS
		}
		region = { region = "Russia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 40 or about once in 2 months
		OR = {
			random = 20 
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 48
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 53
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 57
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 62
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 66
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 48
			}

				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 28

				lost_national = { value = 10 }

			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 42

				lost_national = { value = 20 }

			}
		}
	}

event = {
    id = 4002
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = RUS } }
			ispuppet = RUS
		}
		region = { region = "Russia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 40 or about once in 2 months
		OR = {
			random = 20 
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 48
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 53
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 57
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 62
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 66
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 48
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 48
			}
		}
	}

    id = 4003
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = BLR } }
			ispuppet = BLR
		}
		OR = {
			region 	= { region = "Belarus" 					country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area 	= { area = "Polotsk" 					country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area 	= { area = "Brest Litovsk" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
		}

			random = 5

    id = 4004
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = UKR } }
			ispuppet = UKR
		}
		region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 50
		OR = {
			random = 4 
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 8
			}

				random = 10
			}

			}

				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }

				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }

				random = 6

	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = GRE } }

event = {
    id = 4006
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = POL } }
			ispuppet = POL
		}
		region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 20
		OR = {
			random = 4
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 11
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 13
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 17
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 18
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 10
			}

				dissent = 20
				random = 11

			}

	}

    id = 4007
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		#OR = {
		#	NOT = { alliance = { country = SOV } }
		#	ispuppet = SOV
		#}
		region = { region = "Kaukasus" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		OR = {
			random = 4 
			AND = {
				NOT = { alliance = { country = TUR } }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Kaukasus" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Kaukasus" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Kaukasus" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Kaukasus" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 11
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Kaukasus" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 13
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Kaukasus" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 11
			}

	}

event = {
    id = 4009
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = SPA } }
			ispuppet = SPA
			random = 15 #limit to 15
		}
		region 	= { region = "Spain" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 5  }
		OR = {
			random = 5 
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 19
			}

				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 23
			}

				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 27

				random = 30
			}

	}

#Portugal
event = {
    id = 4010
    random = no
	country = GER
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = POR } }
			ispuppet = POR
			random = 15 #limit to 15
		}
		
		region 	= { region = "Portugal" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 5  }
		OR = {
			random = 5 
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 34
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 37
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 40
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 30
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "Partisans in Portugal!"
	desc = "Partisans in Portugal!"
	
	action = {
		name = "Revolt in Porto!"

#Caucasus SOV version
event = {
    id = 4050
    random = no
	country = SOV
    persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { event = 3183521 } #Chechens deported
		OR = {
			year = 1941
			war = { country = FIN } #Chechen uprising was inspired by the Finnish resistance
		}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 7313 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 1847 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 2368 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 1844 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 3438 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 3437 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 7309 data = -3 }
		}
		OR = {
			random = 5
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 30
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "Partisans in the Caucasus!"
	desc = "Partisans in the Caucasus!"
	
	action = {
		name = "Revolt in Grozny!"
		trigger = { OR = { control = { province = 1847 data = -3 } control = { province = 1847 data = REB } } }
		ai_chance = 20 
 		command = { type = revolt which = 1847 when = 1 }

		command = { type = setflag which = revolt_count_CHE when = 1 value = 1 }
 	}
	action = {

#Yugoslavia
event = {
    id = 4500
    random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = YugRevolt }
		#war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = YUG }
			ispuppet = YUG
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U27 }
			lost_VP = { country = U27 value = 100 } #the exploiting bastard refuses to annex
			NOT = { region = { region = "Balkan" country = U27 data = 1 value = 2 } }
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Croatia" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "Bosnia" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "Central Serbia"		data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "East Serbia"			data = 1 value = 50 }
			war = { country = GER country = SER }
			war = { country = GER country = CRO }
			AND = {
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = ROM country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U30 country = SOV }
					region = { region = "Romania" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = BUL country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U29 country = SOV }
					area = { area = "Bulgaria" 		country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
			}		
		}
		#random = 20
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }

event = {
    id = 4510
    random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = ALBRevolt }
		war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = ALB }
			ispuppet = ALB
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U28 }
			lost_VP = { country = U28 value = 100 } #the exploiting bastard refuses to annex
			#NOT = { region = { region = "Balkan" country = U28 data = 1 value = 1 } }
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Albania" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			AND = {
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = ROM country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U30 country = SOV }
					region = { region = "Romania" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = BUL country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U29 country = SOV }
					area = { area = "Bulgaria" 		country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					area = { area = "East Serbia" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 50 }
					area = { area = "East Serbia" 	country = U27	data = 1 value = 50 }
					random = 2
				}
			}
		}
		#random = 20
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = ALBRevolt }
		
		command = { trigger = { exists = U28 } type = inherit which = U28 value = 1 } #keeping any units if remaining
		
		#Partisan peak strength in 1944 is ~50,000. Most rebel divisions start very understrength
		command = { type = revolt which = 664	when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 664	when = 1 }

		command = { type = revolt which = 390	when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 391	when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 5465	when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 7163	when = 1 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 4511 where = REB when = 8 }
	}
}

#Baltic
event = {
    id = 4590
    random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = BALRevolt }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { exists = LIT }
				NOT = { exists = LAT }
				NOT = { exists = EST }
			}
			ispuppet = LIT
			ispuppet = LAT
			ispuppet = EST
			AND = {
				NOT = { area = { area = "Estonia" 		country = EST data = 1 value = 1 } }
				NOT = { area = { area = "Latvia" 		country = LAT data = 1 value = 1 } }
				NOT = { area = { area = "Lithuania" 	country = LIT data = 1 value = 1 } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = "Baltic" 	country = REB data = 1 value = 35 } #10 provinces
			garrison = { province = 197		country = REB type = land size = 25 area = 2 } #25 rebel divisions in Baltic region
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = BALRevolt }
		
		#keeping any units if remaining
		command = { trigger = { exists = EST } type = inherit which = EST value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { exists = LAT } type = inherit which = LAT value = 1 }

#Manchuria

event = {
	id = 5500
	random = no
	country = MAN
	persistent = yes

	name = "Major revolt in Manchuria"
	desc = "As the Japanese withdrew the bulk of their forces from Manchuria to confront threats in other parts of the world, the Chinese partisans used this opportunity to reorganize in the countryside and launch a new major insurgency against the government of Manchukuo. The Japanese and Manchurian Armies both need to keep at least 15 divisions in Manchuria at all times (30 divisions total) to contain major revolts."
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		NOT = { ai = JAP }
		NOT = { flag = ManRevoltCooldown }
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Jilin" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { area = "Heilongjiang" 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { area = "Liaoning" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { area = "Rehe" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { garrison = { country = MAN province = 2241 type = land size = 15 area = yes } }
			NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 2241 type = land size = 15 area = yes } }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = march year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 20 month = november year = 1945 }

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_DAMN"
		command = { type = dissent 		 value = 0.5  }
		command = { type = manpowerpool  value = -5   }
		command = { type = supplies 	 value = -500 }
		
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		
		command = { type = setflag which = ManRevoltCooldown }
		command = { type = event which = 5501 where = REB when = 20 } #at least 20 days in between revolts
		command = { type = event which = 5502 where = JAP when = 0  }
	}
}

	country = ITA

We need to keep at least 10 Italian divisions in East Africa at all times to contain major revolts."
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		NOT = { ai = ITA }
		NOT = { war = { country = ETH } }
		NOT = { flag = IEARevoltCooldown }
		region = { region = "Ethiopia" 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
		NOT = { garrison = { country = ITA province = 1048 type = land size = 10 area = yes } }
		NOT = { garrison = { country = ITA province = 1033 type = land size = 10 area = yes } }
		NOT = { garrison = { country = ITA province = 1056 type = land size = 10 area = yes } }
	}

	deathdate = { day = 20 month = november year = 1945 }

		name = "ACTION_NAME_DAMN"
		ai_chance = 50
		command = { type = dissent		value = 0.5  }
		command = { type = manpowerpool	value = -5   }
		command = { type = supplies		value = -500 }
		
		command = { type = revolt which = 4680 when = 1 }
		#command = { type = revolt which = 7446 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = 4679 when = 1 }
		#command = { type = revolt which = 1048 when = 1 }

event = {
	id = 5527
	random = no
	country = SOV

	name = "UPA revolt in Western Ukraine"
	desc = "As the Germans withdrew their forces westward, they left behind a number of insurgent militias who benefitted directly or indirectly from the occupation. Aside from the Partisans, most of them did not look kindly at the Soviet government. One of such militias was UPA, formed from the merger of two Ukrainian nationalist organizations OUN-B and OUN-M. OUN attempted to resist the restoration of Soviet rule in western Ukraine and waged an active insurgency for about a year until the dominant presense of the Soviet security apparatus forced them to significantly scale back their activities. UPA then switched to a low level insurgency which continued into the 1950s until the organization was completely suppressed."
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		NOT = { war = { country = UKR } }
		NOT = { flag = UPARevoltCooldown }
		flag = { which = revolt_count_UKR when = 1 value = 10  } #Germany occupied Ukraine at least for some time
		area = { area = "Brest Litovsk" 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
		area = { area = "Lvov"			 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
		#it's okay if some territory is still held by Germany, since UPA attacked Germans too
		#On the other hand, if the frontline is too close, the soviet army will easily suppress the revolt
		random = 30
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = march year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 20 month = november year = 1945 }

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_DAMN"
		command = { type = dissent		value = 0.5  }

		command = { type = setflag which = BALRevoltCooldown }
		command = { type = event which = 5536 where = REB when = 20 } #at least 20 days in between revolts
	}
}

Aside from the Partisans, most of them did not look kindly at the Soviet government. Many such highly autonomous insurgent groups operated in the Baltic States, collectively reffered to as the 'Forest Brothers'. These groups attempted to resist the restoration of Soviet rule in the Baltic and waged an active insurgency for about a year until the dominant presense of the Soviet security apparatus forced them to significantly scale back their activities. The Baltic rebels then switched to a low level insurgency which continued into the 1950s until they were completely suppressed."
